# Linux

Every project across all 39 lists that runs on Linux — **10,911** of 11,993 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

Confirmed first, then inferred, each block by stars. Nearly every container and CI path implies Linux, so this is the widest of the four — filter on Conf. to tighten it.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md) · [7,863 inferred →](linux-inferred.md)

Page **4** of 5, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](linux.md) · [2](linux-2.md) · [3](linux-3.md) · **4** · [5](linux-5.md) — [continue on page 5 →](linux-5.md)

Snapshot 2026-11-28. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

Filter the same data in the browser on the [Atlas site](https://aaa.jeremyfhall.com/catalog/). Spreadsheet edition: retired. The last copy, built 2026-10-22, stays available but is no longer updated. The website is the maintained edition. Its last workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
